That's honestly enough to rattle me, but yeah, nothing besides that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any thoughts or beliefs that other people don't seem to understand, or that you know others might find strange or untrue?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't had anything like that. I mean, I know hearing the voice is strange, but my actual thoughts? No. I don't have any weird beliefs or ideas that people don't understand. I just go to work, do my stocking, and come home. My thoughts are just normal, everyday stuff. Like I said before, my thoughts are pretty normal. I don't have any strange ideas or beliefs that people would think are weird. I just focus on my work at the store and getting through the day. The only thing that's unusual is hearing my name, but my thoughts themselves are just... regular.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any beliefs or ideas that you know are not shared by others, or that you think others might consider untrue or strange, even if you haven’t talked about them?\n\nPatient: I think you just asked me that, but... no. I really don't. I don't have any secret ideas I'm keeping to myself, or anything like that. I'm just a regular person trying to get through my day. Like I said, I just focus on my job and my normal life. There's really nothing else going on in my head besides hearing my name.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any thoughts or experiences that you’ve kept to yourself because you were worried others wouldn’t understand or might think they’re strange?\n\nPatient: Honestly, doctor, I feel like we're going in circles here. I haven't kept anything hidden. I don't have any strange thoughts or experiences that I'm worried about sharing. Like I've been saying, the only thing that's bothering me is hearing my name when no one is there. Other than that, everything is just normal.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any beliefs or ideas that you think might not be true, but you still feel strongly about them even when others say they aren't real or reasonable?\n\nPatient: Doctor, I... I really don't know how else to say it. No. I don't have any beliefs like that. I'm not holding onto anything that isn't real. I just go to work, stock the shelves, and come home. That's it. I'm not sure why you keep asking me this same kind of question, but the answer is still no.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any experiences where you strongly believed something was true, even though others told you it wasn’t, and later you realized they were right?\n\nPatient: Look, doctor... no. I really haven't. I know you probably have a checklist of things you need to ask, but I've never had anything like that happen. I've never been convinced of something that turned out to be completely wrong or made up. Like I said, it's just hearing my name when nobody is there. That's the only unusual thing going on with me.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
